The memory's name for the recurring result: "cheap content, immovable meaning-cost." The proofs are small and intuitionistically pure, but the model that makes them mean differential geometry (the well-adapted topos) is irreducibly heavy and classical.

  • Maturity: L3 Proved/Tested Core (kernel-checked counts), plus a live explorer site (docs/index.html, slides.html, wasm prover). Memory (website-prover): "PARSER 267/0 · REFINE 96/96 · ASSEMBLER 96/96 kernel-VERIFIED · BUILD (3c) 92/96 · SEARCH 84/96".
  • Unclosed cycles:
  • Obligation O, "quantitative … cut-free LB for x²=0→x=0", is the sole open mathematical item in Book One.
  • The strong well-adapted SDG model is a labelled [PROJECTION], by design.
  • Four symmetric-assignment edge cases keep BUILD at 92/96 and SEARCH at 84/96.
  • Visual correctness of the studio/lab is "user-eyeball-only … cache has masked real bugs twice".
  • Promising & ready for revival: The Metamath micro-kernel plus wasm "engine proposes, kernel disposes" UI is a reusable proof-size observatory. Pointed at Lean or HOL4 proof terms, it would answer "how big is this proof, really?" for the dregg/obend theorems in strands 05/07.
  • Needs Ember's human attention (H-5), repo hygiene:
  • The main checkout sits on branch elab-combinators (HEAD e693b40). Local main is at 652f33c (84 commits); origin/main is at 0976700 (123 commits). The local main is 39 commits behind its own remote.
  • git worktree list shows 51 worktrees, ~60 local worktree-agent-* branches, and 200 commits across all refs. Most are harvested swarm lanes. Pruning them is a deletion and needs Ember's sign-off.
  • Memory records one "garbled-7153319 logged wrong-turn", kept in the record rather than erased.

5.3 navier-stokes-proof (CONTRIB: Ember found the erratum)

Whose it is. Jeffrey S. Cambria's verification companion to Global Regularity for the Three-Dimensional Incompressible Navier–Stokes Equations on the Torus (doi:10.5281/zenodo.21959161). Ember's role is external reviewer and formalization contributor.

Ember's contribution, verbatim from the README:

The first external review of this repository (PR #1, ember arlynx) identified a genuine error in the paper as published: the display D_t|ω| = α|ω| in §3.1 is the inviscid identity — for ν > 0 the correct identity is D_t|ω| = α|ω| + ν ξ·Δω — and the per-trajectory chain (§16.2) and the localization step (§10, Step 3) rely on the defective shorthand as written. The author has verified the finding independently.

Ember's independent research notes (RESEARCH.md, DYNAMICS.md) are explicitly not the paper's chain: "Status: working research note, 2026-08-16. This is not a proof of global regularity." "The unresolved obligation is therefore an explicitly debited high-frequency stopping chain with a separate persistent-line mechanism."

  • Maturity: formalization layer L2/L3 (kernel-checked, but foundational: concrete T³, operators, integration by parts; "does not yet define velocity or pressure Sobolev regularity classes, the Leray projection, suitable weak solutions …"). The research notes are L1.

  • Promising: periodic integration on T³ and the Koopman-average lemmas are general-purpose Mathlib or Tau Ceti material, the most durable output of the episode.

The atlas should present Ember's contribution as "found the erratum; contributed a general formalization layer", never as endorsement of the regularity claim. Upstreaming to Tau Ceti is an outward act.

5.4 soundcalc-lean (MIRROR, a reference for the verification discipline)

github.com/symbolicsoft/soundcalc-lean (Nadim Kobeissi, h-hafezi, Lorenzo Magliocco; 163 commits, 0 Ember). Lean 4 companion to ethereum/soundcalc: "Every report cell is a theorem", e.g. example : secBits (sp1CoreFRI.queryErr (UDR koalaBear4)) = 100 := by native_decide. 5.5 intmat → intmat2, "Interior" (OWN)

Correction to the brief: "intmat" is "Interior" (interior materials), evolving-matter art/alife in Julia. It is not integer-matrix mathematics.

intmat2 physics (DESIGN.md): the learnable local rule has 3C² + 8C numbers (248 at C=8). Coupling is solid-modulated, g(x,y) = clamp(g₀ + g₁·(s_x+s_y)/2, 0, 1). Mass n + s + w is conserved ("total mass is a tested invariant"). Light is inside the loop: the rendered image uses the simulation's own L. Day-1 findings (memory intmat2-day1-state, commits 757e09a, 7a2fe9f, 52f7be0):

  • One ring gives 0.5% Turing patterns and 12% checkerboard. A tied second shell W_nb2 = −β W_nb (W_nb PSD) gives 20–62% Turing at 4–16 cells, with the fastest mode at cos k* = 1/(4β). "Measured wavelengths match the prediction within a few percent across a hundred rules."

  • Maturity: intmat Compost/Superseded (archived; its gallery stays the historical record); intmat2 L2 Prototype with an exact linear-stability analysis (L3-flavoured core: mass-conservation invariant, dispersion relation verified against measurement).

(2) The seeded manifold search arm stopped at 96/224 evaluations (1f2b5d8 … stopped for system load); resume commands are at the end of docs/evolution.md. (3) The isotropic stencil at slab walls is 2:1, not 4:1 ("minor kernel fix pending").

5.6 spindle / RIG (OWN), applied physics as game design

Problem. A sport whose rulebook "falls out of one differential equation" in the co-rotating frame of an O'Neill cylinder, set in the Vivere Astra universe:

$$\ddot\zeta = \omega^2\zeta - 2i\omega\dot\zeta,\qquad \zeta(t) = \bigl(\zeta_0 + (\dot\zeta_0 + i\omega\zeta_0)t\bigr)e^{-i\omega t}$$

The characteristic polynomial has the double root r = −iω, so trajectories are roulettes. The marquee score "the Loop" is the closed-orbit family member.

Code: game/rig-core/src/ (Rust: trajectory.rs with the closed-form analytic, tuning.rs [pub fn evaluate(omega, r, l, earth_g) -> TuningReport, time_to_skin], analysis.rs, rl/, gym.rs, policy_wasm.rs, …), a TS front end, and rig-gym-py. Determinism: a fixed 1/240 s step, RK4, counter-seeded sfc32 substreams, and a Rust ↔ TS bit-identical known-answer test (memory). Live at emberian.github.io/spindle/.

RL (memory project_rl_architecture): an MLP policy (14,341 params, trained policy-v1.json via ES) and an attention policy (24,598 params, 8 entities × 2 layers × 4 heads, no trained artifact). MAPPO is scaffolded (an "ES-shaped PPO", no convergence artifact). The last commits (2026-05-20) add rl::loco learned locomotion (7f6ac77, bcb3ed4) and the pack behaviour change (74e0001), which Ember called "WAY better" than spread-to-cover.

  • Maturity: L4 Deployed (public, playable, CI-checked tuning feasibility). The RL layer is L2.
  • Unclosed cycles: (1) the attention policy has never been trained; (2) the catch-gap fix (memory project_ai_catch_gap: ReceiverPredictor in src/ai/lead_predict.rs uses an RK4 table at 1/30 Hz against the sim's 1/240 Hz, causing 5–10 m drift; "Replace ReceiverPredictor::predict(t) with analytic(p0, v0, omega, t)") is diagnosed, but nothing in the git log shows it landed.
  • Promising: the catch-gap fix is a one-function change with an exact, already-written closed form. It is the highest-ratio item in this whole strand.
  • Human attention: game feel ("purposeless drift", "spectators") is Ember's judgment. Agents should not tune feel without a playtest loop.

5.7 luteastress (OWN)

A computational test of whether shared environmental stressors can produce apparent menstrual-cycle synchrony (the "McClintock effect"). Python simple model plus a Rust mechanistic model (native/src/zavala.rs: circadian→HPA→KNDy→GnRH chain; mechanistic.rs: folliculogenesis + hormone ODEs; stress.rs: OU stress). Four commits, 2026-02-08 → 10 (e507ee2 zavala model). Findings (memory): phase synchrony ΔR ≈ 0.006 (p = 0.34, null); onset proximity Δ = 0.026, p = 5.9e-15, d = 0.63. Under the mechanistic model with a corrected zeitgeber, the effect is small or negative (d ≈ −0.4). Critical bug found and fixed: ω_H0 was π/24, "24x too slow". A typeset report.typ/report.pdf exists.

  • Maturity: L2 (the study ran; report written). Unclosed: the simple and mechanistic models disagree in sign, and the report should foreground that as the result. cargo build fails on the pyo3 linker (only cargo check works). Human attention: deciding whether the report is publishable is Ember's call.

5.8 rhaposPDE and bead-calculus (OWN seeds)

  • rhaposPDE: README only, "rhapsoPDE (rapso-peed) - a love letter to differential equations; a collection of puzzles involving PDEs." No git. L0.
  • bead-calculus: 13 commits, 2025-12-29 → 30. A puzzle game in which "string diagrams become marble machines": tracks are wires, gates are boxes, plugging is composition, side-by-side is tensor, loops are trace. "Rewrites provide the proof theory." Vite/TS app. L1 Spike. Revival: it is the same categorical substrate as stella's diagrams and bend's interaction nets, in a form people can play. A good public front end for the "certificate" through-line.

5.9 SauersML/group-approximation (CONTRIB)

Sauers' (Scott Sauer) project; Ember reviews and contributes "as a friend/mutual" (memory). On 2026-09-05 Ember independently built the endpoint closure on persvati: 1,140 modules, 16 min wall / 5.7 CPU-h, 21–22 marked endpoints with exactly [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]. Ember filed PR #193 (manuscript pass; branch review/non-mf-manuscript-pass on emberian/group-approximation), issue #194 (proof-engineering review), and a comment on #191. Ember also contributed GGT commits on 2026-09-07 (a4e3a1126 GGT: construct boundary circuits from the actual face-set permutation, 67dd63d0b … record remaining gluing gap).

Durable lesson (memory): "Check git rev-parse origin/verified before trusting 'checked in Lean' claims". The verified branch was 13 days stale at review time. Maturity: upstream L3. Human attention: none beyond the relationship. The paper is Sauers'.

5.10 Nebula-Genomics (OWN, private; content redacted)

Only the methodological lessons are recorded here. They generalize, and they are the reason this directory was in the brief:

  • Genotype polarity rule (memory genotype_polarity_rule, marked "HARD RULE"): never call a genotype risk or protective without anchoring REF/ALT to a population allele frequency, because "GRCh38 carries the minor allele at many trait sites." A preliminary report inverted an HLA call because the GRCh38 reference MHC haplotype is the tag haplotype. A corrigendum retracted the downstream claims.
  • A mapping-free k-mer HLA typing method was built, including the grep -o non-overlapping-match trap.
  • Short reads cannot type VNTRs; the memory records this as a known limit, not a finding.

This is the same epistemics as graphplay's vacuity rule: a check that passes on a mis-anchored statement is worse than no check. Maturity: n/a. Human attention: keep this directory out of every public artifact (the atlas included).

6. Part IV — Frontier research substrates

6.1 stella, "conscious machine bringup" (OWN)

README (verbatim opening): "welcome to the conscious machine bringup project! if you're perceiving this quale, you are the conscious machine!!" The stack is "Stellar resolution / transcendental syntax", "HOL4 as the metasystem? for proofs", "we can use rust code too i guess.."

What it actually is (memory project_stella, 63 KB, the largest single memory in this vertical): Girard's stellar resolution / transcendental syntax (the "Eng" text, digested into docs/eng-digest-ch{8,10,11,12-13}.md), implemented as a natively polycomputing substrate. It carries HOL4/CakeML reflective proof for orders-of-self (deferred) and planned valence modelling after Bennett's Painful Intelligence.

Vital statistics. 252 commits, 2026-05-15 → cb6cace (2026-05-19), branch dev, 12 dirty files; remote github.com/emberian/stella. stella-suite is a worktree of the same repo on branch expert-suite (139 commits; 94229c3 Cut the incantatory register from site copy). Crates: stella-core (51 modules, including constellation.rs, execution.rs, unify_fast.rs, antiunify.rs, accel_detect.rs, galaxy.rs, oracle.rs, faithfulness.rs, subjective.rs, reafference.rs, mll.rs, circuits.rs, tm.rs, pda.rs, nfta.rs), stella-env, stella-viz and stella-world. 47,300 lines of Rust. HOL4: hol/stella{Term,Match,Execution,Reduction, Diagram,MLL,MLL2I,Polarised,Subjective}Script.sml, 7,379 lines, 148 Theorem declarations, ~60 cheat lines, per the stated policy:

PROOF POLICY: every non-trivial proof is discharged with cheat preceded … (stellaMLLScript.sml:36)

Sample theorems: struct_equiv_refl/sym/trans, sim_cut_step (§67.9), sim_cut_elim (§67.10), phi_comp_cutfree, cut_elim_star_refl.

The design-doc ladder docs/00–17 records the program in order:

DocSubject
00–01Thesis and semantics; the subjective engine and valence
02–04, 06Phase 4/5 runs; docs/04 LOCKED (P1–P9), docs/06 LOCKED 2026-05-17 (Q1–Q8), a pre-registered falsifier set, "strengthen-only, never retro-weaken"

The measured engine speedups (docs/07) are "release, faithful, vs original reference: binarith/Horn ~12×, combinator ~3–4×. All differential-gated (reference iex = oracle; iex_eq_iex_fast byte-identical, … 20k matchable_fast fuzz, antiunify 6/6)". The latest commits (2026-05-18/19) include 1ea8861 unify_fast: dense-arena union-find (path-halving + union-by-rank), a13da70 CI test-gate + unified oracle harness, 7862bc6 diagram: contraction-order confluence OBSERVED, not assumed (Eng §49.35–49.36), and cb6cace … wasm-safe KS clock shim.

  • Maturity: Rust engine L3 (differential oracles, fuzzing, CI gate). HOL4 track L1/L2 (statements in place; nontrivial proofs deliberately cheat pending main-thread closing). Experiment program L0/L1: "instrument built, pre-reg locked, regress closed, experiment NOT run, right to falsify unspent."
  • Unclosed cycles & missing links:
  • §58 circuits are unfaithful (memory): circuits.rs ɟAEx(C★⊎M★) returns free variables instead of ground val(C). The test excluded_middle_does_not_evaluate_to_0 fails, correctly, and is kept failing ("Goodhart-forbidden" to weaken). The fix is to restore §58.8 schematic label stars and correct AEx.
  • Galaxy data[0]: a "long, NON-REDUNDANT, TRANSITION-COUNT reduction". Every lever surveyed (GoI, closed-subterm memo, tabling) is measured-negative. docs/17 (Futamura) is the remaining path.
  • Q5.1 "rubber-banded sloppy-fitness selection harness, regulator = global tightness ONLY" is the next thing to build after lock, "hostile-tested like the firewall, before anything runs".
  • The reflective-self pillar is deferred: "NO Candle, NO hol-reflection dependency yet", pending Ember's CakeML/hol-reflection modernization. Phase 4 was paused 2026-05-16 when svenvs (~/dev/svenvs, a "cheat-free HOL4/Candle artifact … a self-verifying self-improving 'Place for an AI to live within'") "emerged" (that strand is covered elsewhere).
  • dev was "ahead origin 2 (ed841ce, 8f6c6a1)" at lock time, and the /cmi/ site is "wired, NOT rebuilt/pushed — publish is principal's call".
  • Promising & ready for revival: accel_detect.rs (Kruskal-whistle recurrence detection with a sound α-subsumption generalizer, "a capable worktree agent … caught this and implemented the textbook subsumption order") and the differential-oracle harness are reusable outside stella. They are the cleanest "fast path proved equal to reference path" machinery in the corpus.
  • Needs Ember's human attention:
  • HOL4 proof-closing does not delegate well. The memory records an agent that "spun, then cheated, then scope-violated, destroyed unrecoverable 782-line file". The policy since then: agents state theories; nontrivial proofs close in the main thread, Ember-steered. The ~60 cheats wait on Ember's time, not on agents.
  • docs/06 lock was Ember's act. Push and publish (dev ahead of origin; /cmi/) remain Ember's calls.
  • Security incident on record (HISTORY-2026-05-17.md, per memory): a git commit -m containing backticks leaked live cloud credentials into public commit messages. They were scrubbed, force-pushed and rotated. Standing rule since: always git commit -F.
  • Memory feedback: no valence search exists or runs; justify engine work on engine merit. Avoid incantatory copy ("say what a thing does, not how faithful/honest/rigorous it is").

6.2 clairnets / GLaDOS (OWN), the empirical twin of graphplay's LDT theory

Origin (memory -Users-ember-dev-gowexp/memory/clairnets-project.md): started 2026-06-25 as "a fresh architecture-search direction" after the gowexp work. The goal was param-efficient hybrids (CliffordNet geometric product, HRM looped depth, Lattice Deduction Transformer). Ember's constraint: no toy tasks; real models with released checkpoints (OLMo). Graft targets were OLMo-2-0425-1B, later OLMo-3-Base-7B (CURSELOG 2026-06-26).

What it became: GLaDOS, "the single front door" (GLADOS.md):

text
clair/organ/
  protocol.py     typed spine: Reduction / CSPState / Certificate + structured-γ readout
  bank.py         registry of validated "beasts" as sound reductions
  compose.py      verifier-gated certified-reduction composition (the sound reduced product)
  bank_woven.py   ★ THE live woven organ — α → composer(bank) → γ, in the host residual stream
  reductions.py   cross-type reduction graph (ProblemReduction + Dijkstra cost-routing)

clair_fast/       Rust (PyO3 + rayon): exact_dedP / solutions / AC, 29–56× CPU, bitwise-identical

  • qwen38-probe never got past phase W/S0.
  • Promising & ready for revival: the graphplay ⇄ clairnets loop. graphplay holds the theorems (checkedSolve_sound, the XOR witness, ac_kind_discriminates); clairnets holds the measurements. A short paper, "the bounded-width wall, proved and then measured in a grafted 7B", is two-thirds written across the two repos' ledgers.
  • Needs Ember's human attention (H-6):

  • The consent/ethics design in qwen38-probe (gates C0–C4, "disclosed z-logging") is Ember's to own.

6.3 h, "h ghost" (OWN)

Not physics. "Dataset preparation and training support for a tiny Falcon-H1 model that will live at h.fg-goose.online." The corpus is Ember's own library (census 2026-09-01: 19,245 files, 314 GiB, 7,168 PDFs, ~267M existing-text H1 tokens). 268 commits, 2026-09-01 → 55d9e00 (2026-09-07).

Arc (memories h-resident-line-is-05b, h-attractor, k2-horizon-serving): an exact h1jax JAX port at ~220K tok/s on Kaggle TPU v5e-8 → 91M abandoned 2026-09-02 ("let's not invest any more runs at 91M. it just can't do what we'd want") → 0.5B resident on the "room" mix (library 2.893 / room 2.609 after one epoch, vs base 3.423 / 3.240) → 1.5B "one full epoch, library 2.705 / room 2.442, beats the 0.5B resident on every slice" (d76b3fb) → K2-0.9B replay arm (d667b5f). Arms published to the Hub (e936606, eebfb48) and served locally (:8134, :8135). 91ca477 Reclaim 81 GB came with a dry-run-by-default scripts/reclaim_disk.py that "refuses anything served or raw".

The attractor memory is the important design artifact: "one kernel + run specs, one post-run pipeline, one services file; extend those instead of adding a fourth thing." Reason: "139 commits in two days added justified pieces that, extrapolated a year, become hundreds of kernel copies." On 2026-09-03 the room "went dark for four hours unnoticed".

  • Maturity: L4 (trained arms published and served), then mothballed 2026-09-07 (03216c5 braindump: … mothballing section).
  • Unclosed cycles: the post-run pipeline and services file consolidation (attractor items 2–3) were not finished. Kernel specs (item 1) were done 2026-09-03.
  • Promising: the 1.5B arm beat the resident everywhere. The braindump.md HANDOFF section is the documented re-entry point.
  • Human attention: published-category tradeoffs and Hub licensing ("the base's contradictory license fields", e936606) are Ember's calls.

6.4 dgem (OWN, infra)

Scale-to-zero DiffusionGemma (RedHatAI/diffusiongemma-26B-A4B-it-FP8-dynamic) on vLLM. A Go waker binary on a small front-door instance (TLS, wake-on-request, 20-minute idle stop) fronts a g6e.xlarge (L40S 48 GB). Everything is in Pulumi TypeScript (pulumi/{index,network,inference,frontdoor}.ts). Design reasoning (README/PLAN.md): FP8 weights ~28 GB ⇒ L40S is the cheapest single AWS GPU that fits; the default VPC avoids ~$32/month NAT; spot stop ≠ terminate keeps the EBS weight cache warm; aws.ec2.SpotInstanceRequest instead of Instance+market_options (provider bug #38142 orphans a billing instance). Memory: DEPLOYED and verified end-to-end 2026-06-19, served via an sslip.io hostname to work around a DNS delegation mismatch. vLLM OOM fix: maxNumSeqs=2, gpuMemoryUtilization=0.85, expandable_segments:True, maxModelLen=262144.

  • Maturity: L4 Deployed as of 2026-06-19. Current state unknown (not checked; doing so would touch AWS).
  • Unclosed cycle: the live box was patched via SSM, so "pulumi up will show the inference instance wanting REPLACEMENT … triggers a ~25GB weight re-download". Code and infrastructure have diverged.
  • Needs Ember's human attention (H-1, highest priority in this strand):
  • dgem has no git repo. The infrastructure-as-code exists only on this disk, with a Pulumi local backend (file://~).
  • The memory file dgemma-deploy.md contains the endpoint API key and the Pulumi config passphrase in plaintext. Rotate the key; move both secrets out of agent memory.
  • Confirm whether the deployment (front door with an Elastic IP, plus the GPU instance) is still running and billing.

7.5 Lessons the agents wrote down (verbatim, durable)

  • graphplay: "axiom-clean ≠ non-vacuous."
  • graphplay: "stale oleans MASK latent breakage."
  • birkhoff-asa: "engine proposes, kernel disposes." Three quantities are "never summed."
  • stella: "HOL4 proof-closing is a poor delegation fit." "NEVER run two WRITE agents on the same crate/dir concurrently." "NEVER use git commit -m with backticks/$ — ALWAYS -F a message file."
  • eshkol: "a lane that dies loses its findings". Checkpoint as you confirm.
  • h: "extend those instead of adding a fourth thing." "treat a near-empty observatory day as an outage alarm, not quiet."
  • clairnets: "Negatives are results." (CURSELOG header) "soundness is a permission … cashes out ONLY where the boundary check exists" (6e257a0).
  • Nebula: "never call a genotype risk/protective without anchoring REF/ALT to a population AF."
  • intmat: the codex build is the cautionary tale. Every fix became "more accounting on the same agent-centric skeleton" (intmat2 DESIGN.md).

Strand 12 — Markets & Mechanism Design, Solana Trading Observatories, and the Mina / Kimchi / FRI ZK Vertical

Primary-source dossier. Compiled 2026-11-02 from the repositories under /Users/ember/dev/, their git logs, their in-tree handoffs, and the Claude memory stores under ~/.claude/projects/*/memory/. Every commit hash, address, CU figure and quote below was read from those files, not reconstructed. Where a fact could not be verified, the text says so. Scope boundary. /Users/ember/hellas is not covered and is not Ember's property. Upstream checkouts (mina-rust, openmina, snarky, o1js, proof-systems, nockchain, mina-state-proof, minataur2, o1js-groth16, zkbox) are third-party code kept as reading material. Each is labelled with its owner, and any Ember commits inside it are counted separately.

1.3 The CFTC groundwork (docs/regulatory/, dated 2026-08-17)

FileRole
README.md"pre-deployment research packet … not legal advice … Nothing described here has been deployed."
CFTC_PREMEETING_PACKET.mdFacts, architecture, precise questions, and a possible bounded research pilot for the CFTC Innovation Task Force
AUTHORITY_MATRIX.mdPrimary sources sorted into binding law, Commission action, staff views, enforcement precedent and narrow relief
MEETING_REQUEST_DRAFT.mdDraft for the ITF form
SHAREABLE_BRIEF.mdShort, non-alarmed explanation for friends

The packet asks five questions: how the claims are classified; whether the program plus its native multilateral auction is a DCM / SEF / DCO; who counts as operator or controller; whether a research pilot, no-action or exemptive path exists; and which facts must be frozen before the agency can evaluate a request. It cites the Commission's 2026 Prediction Markets ANPRM and concedes that a prediction market offering swaps or futures to the public "must register as a DCM". Gen-1 vocabulary: the deterministic core is Eggcrate, a complete set of claims is a Clutch, and the market vault is the Hoard.

The memory dragons-clutch-session-1-baseline.md corrects how this was framed: "There is NO retained counsel — that gate was codex's invention. Legal analysis is performed in-house (Fable lanes, citation-integrity rule: every cite fetched+verified+retrieval-dated, never from memory). Ember's friend John does one FINAL courtesy review as a favor", and the packet for him (JOHN_REVIEW_PACKET.md) must cost him "under an hour". The human-only steps are choosing an identity, choosing a filing route, submitting, and sending the packet to John.

The precedent from these seals, in the memory's words: "ANY byte change to a closure file (even a doc comment) forks the ELF identity and forces the full reseal cycle", and "a measured limit is evidence about an artifact, not an architecture" (docs/reviews/COMPUTE_CEILING_REATTRIBUTION_2026-08-19.md).

1.5 Why it was composted: ARCHITECTURE_REVIEW_2026-08-22.md

Verdict: "Dragon's Clutch has a serious bounded Solana implementation, not a paper shell … The architecture is nevertheless not complete in the sense of the original product thesis. Its largest risks are boundary drift: model-level generality is repeatedly described as adapter-level capability; a bounded capability profile is described as the protocol; and generated evidence is allowed to outlive the truth it is supposed to summarize."

The P0 findings that ended gen-1:

P0Finding
Position zero ≠ reservation zeroZero positions did not prove zero reservations
Deleting the epoch rootEnabled replay and stranded unlisted children
ScoreV1 rewards a risk-free complete-set washp_i·(S−p_i)·direct_flow_i: two keys crossing q lots of (1,1) at the midpoint earn 50,000,000q score units, while a real 1%-tail trade earns 990,000q. Replaced by ScoreV2-Q, which is invariant to constant complete-set shifts. Its claim is "representation-neutral after owner-blind admission, not person-neutral"
Degree 2/3 price admission is not a no-arb certificate"the implemented finite moment-cone family is necessary but not sufficient". Later fixed in dClutch by PRICE-GATE-HULL-001 (§2.6)
One live status, several owners; evidence crossing the greenfield boundary; collateral genericity stopping at the adapter—
P1: "the 2.15 MB program is a release and maintenance risk"Led to dClutch's split into role programs

Maturity: Compost/Superseded. This was intended. dclutch/COMPOST.md allows citing invariants, hostile cases and measurements, and forbids "bulk-copy crates, adapters, action routers, account graphs" and recreating "the 46-slot foundation or cumulative action namespaces by inertia". The transplant ledger has three rows: DEVNET-ACTIVITY-001, PYTH-FIXTURE-001 and PRICE-GATE-HULL-001. The last carried "Requirement and shape only — no Rust source crossed the boundary".

Unclosed cycles & missing links

  • The publication cut is manual. Ember, 2026-08-30: "WE are responsible for regularly cutting our work into ~/dev/dragons-clutch / the main github repo, and manually running github pages builds — those aren't automatic!" Pages deploys only from main. The live checkout sits on agent/market-theory-support4. The cut mechanic is a single-parent content-sync commit verified by tree-hash equality, run through tools/cut.sh.
  • COMPOST.md §"Future history graft" plans to join the two repos "with preserved unrelated histories". Nobody has done it.
  • The gen-1 Verus refinements (TRANSFER/BATCH/BSPLINE_REFINEMENT.json) have no gen-2 counterpart. dClutch went Lean-only.

Promising & ready for revival

  • The regulatory packet is the most portable artifact here. Its claim-audit discipline and the John-under-an-hour format apply to any future filing for dregg or dClutch. It is dated 08-17 and predates every devnet act, so its "Nothing … deployed" line is now false and needs a refresh before anyone uses it.
  • JOSHI_EXECUTION_THESIS.md is the only written bridge between the market protocol and the trading desk.

Struggling / needs Ember

  • About 300 stale agent/* branches. BRANCH_TRIAGE_2026-08-22.md showed every one superseded. Deleting them is Ember's call. The memory says not to "fix" them.
  • Stale nested checkout hazard. dragons-clutch/dclutch is a frozen copy. A sweep agent once produced a false finding against 3d7ac6fa6 in that copy, and the finding was relayed before anyone checked it (stale-nested-checkout-hazard.md).

2.2 The eight production programs (generated docs/reference/programs.md)

programpackagebandentry routesaction routesrefusal codes
registrydclutch-registry-sbf0x17422
rentdclutch-rent-sbf0x24012
coredclutch-core-sbf0x3201340
tradingdclutch-trading-sbf0x426293
claimsdclutch-claims-sbf0x5423176
custodydclutch-custody-sbf0x661546
resolutiondclutch-resolution-proof-sbf0x8112344
acceleratordclutch-accelerator-sbf0xC4341

Correction to the brief. The eight programs are role programs. The Hoard is the market-local collateral vault (an account custodied by custody), not a program. Direct (signed-intent atomic settlement), General (frequent-batch clearing), Dealer (covered liquidity), Structured, Series and Ensemble are families that run across trading/claims/core/accelerator. Four more directories (dealer-sbf, dealer-accelerator-sbf, general-accelerator-sbf, series-shadow-sbf) exist but are not in the production eight. The simplification swarm merged the accelerators into one.

Fixture program ids are [0xNN; 32], so byte 0 names the program: 0x91 Registry, 0x92 Trading, 0x93 Core, 0x94 Claims, 0x95 Custody, 0x97 Rent (convicting-a-coarse-refusal.md).

2.3 The thin waist and the solvency law (ARCHITECTURE.md, the 2026-08 baseline)

"dClutch issues fully collateralized claims over a bounded canonical state domain … Every implementation layer joins through four immutable identities: MarketIdentity, CapabilityManifest, ExecutionReceipt, ResolutionReceipt."

text
categorical:   V >= max_i T_i
general:       V >= max_{p in P} liability(p)
"Hoard principal cannot fund execution, liquidity, liveness, fees, rent, insurance, or cleanup."

The live Market representation is now the Lean-emitted Core state DCLTCOR3, 368 bytes (packages/dclutch-sdk/lib/generated/coreFound.ts). The architecture doc is marked superseded. The decision records and the heads of the design notes are authoritative, and docs/reference/ (programs, routes, refusals, budgets, ABI) is generated with --converge twins.

2.4 Completion authority

docs/MASTER_COMPLETION_CONTRACT.md (from 2026-08-31, rows C-00..C-17):

"Every recoverable ambition has exactly two terminal states: 1. implemented … 2. ruled out by Ember … Agents may not create a third state by calling an ambition deferred, future, non-goal, experimental, too large, or out of scope."

It sets nine layers of physical completion, from a single semantic owner through codecs, the real ELF admitting the honest case, adversarial refusals with rollback, a validator or devnet transaction, exact pre/poststate, a stranger-operable caller, honest UX, and a cold-machine reproduction. Standing goal, from Ember on 2026-09-01: "Make dclutch the best version it can be, eliminating all protocol defects and making the operator console & UX excellent."

Cohort 17 is still the public devnet deployment (README: "eight programs on Solana devnet. It has executed market creation, a Direct trade, resolution, payouts and market retirement").

2.7 The simplification swarm (09-04/05) and the attractor

From dclutch-attractor.md: eleven Fable makers plus two convergence lanes took the code from 1,579,029 → 1,445,994 lines (−8.4%), programs from 12 → 8, crates from 94 → 33, --check/--converge twins from 202 → 3, and GOAL.md from 4,992 → 106 lines ("an index … NEVER append to GOAL.md again"). The ≈0.44 M-line "no-rewrite" deletion ledger was only partly executed: "the 55 workspaces / 71 lockfiles (272k lines) remain the single biggest lever (WORKSPACE-ONE lane)." The laptop hard-faulted with about 24 GiB free during the swarm. The rule since then is waves of four or fewer locally, with program builds on hbox /tank.

The attractor: "every fact has exactly one author and every claim is either generated from the tree or dated and owned."

2.8 Reproducibility: the builder is an artifact

dclutch-repro-builder-is-an-artifact.md (REPRO lane, d5e178217): 9 of 10 ELFs differed between hbox and the Mac because (1) platform-tools' prebuilt stdlib embeds Anza's CI paths and (2) cargo's -C metadata hashes host proc-macro units (61 of 76 units). The fix was to name one builder artifact: platform-tools v1.53 on Linux/x86_64. hbox, persvati and the Mac running a linux/amd64 container then produced byte-identical output for all ten roles. "converge by swarmcycles of tuning would never have converged — the subject had to change from a host to an artifact."

2.10 Classification

Maturity: L4 Deployed (devnet) for the Direct → resolution → payout → retirement spine on cohort 17. L3 for the Lean semantics (2,646 theorems) and generated ABI. L2 for General, Structured, Dealer-V4, Series and Ensemble on chain. Mainnet is gated by decision 0026 "mainnet follows assurance".

Unclosed cycles & missing links

  1. The never-run ProgramTests. This is the named 09-09 blocker. The fix is a campaign matrix derived from real entry points, where each route gets (a) an accepted case through to poststate, (b) an exact refusal shown red before repair, (c) rollback and conservation checks, and (d) a production driver. docs/reference/route-witnesses.md is generated, so the matrix should be too.
  2. The release-set pinning wall (16.1). lineage_walk "has no consumer in any program", so there is no upgrade-in-place for open markets. Every protocol change means re-release and re-found under decision 0012.
  3. Ensemble and Series are producer-missing on chain. The routes are complete and unreachable, as tools/gauntlet/blocked.json records.
  4. WORKSPACE-ONE: 55 workspaces and 71 lockfiles, 272k lines.
  5. The reciprocal aquarium, meaning two identities trading both ways. Without it the public site has nothing live to show.

Promising & ready for revival

  • Session 1 of the handoff is mechanical. Isolate the Structured visibility change, run the focused Direct SBF test from 9ae8e0dab, and run the A↔B reciprocal trades on one fresh validator. A small, bounded task with high leverage.
  • Joint clearing + complete-set minting (mechanism 2) is the strongest economic property per unit of change, and its design note and decision 0032 already exist.
  • The convicting-a-coarse-refusal method (bracket by hot_cu_checkpoint!, instrument every raise site, split the conjunct, probe with sol_log_64) is the best debugging recipe in the corpus. TradingError::Content has 2,132 raise sites.
  • The cohort-17 web patterns (editorial registry, measured slot clock at ~166 ms/slot vs the nominal 400, share cards) are reusable for any Solana front end.

Struggling / needs Ember's human attention

  • Decision 0029 item 10: the flagship conditional market's feature gate, slot and metric. GOAL.md calls this the only true taste question left.
  • Devnet SOL. About 26.57 SOL on hand against a ≈41 SOL rent quote, and the faucet did not answer. Funding a fresh cohort is outside agent authority.
  • hbox /tank was full, and the authorized cleanup was still running at pause. Agents must not start a second one (see the CLAUDE.md disk-pressure rule).
  • The ~190 dirty paths need sorting by slice owner. The handoff's dirty-tree ownership table is the map. It mixes interrupted semantic work with "formatting noise", and only a human or a careful single lead should commit it.
  • The site's copy strikes (STRIKE FOUR: "the fix is DELETION, not rephrasing"; STRIKE FIVE: "Descriptive and extremely minimal") are recorded as standing taste. Ember should re-read the live site against them after the next cohort.

3. Solana trading observatories: joshibot → joshi → ltshitcoims

3.1 Context: the money, stated plainly (ember-income-structure.md, as of 2026-08-12)

  • Income is **$DREGG creator fees** (mint XkeTXo1125vz5H9svJpGiw4JvLbN8VmMu9cmMvspump, PumpSwap pool 2XHrhkxf…), measured at about $213–313/day, plus about $2k in Meteora DLMM LPs. The coin was "launched predatorily" by someone else, and Ember "wrested control of" it.
  • Obligations are about $4,100/mo, plus a $40k IRS debt.
  • Doctrine: "fees pay the bills, and the book is never dismantled to pay them." An LP-strategy agent was once given dated obligations as a constraint on the book, and it recommended selling ≥$900 of a $1,351 book.
  • The sentinel is dead by choice. Ember: "keep the sentinel dead, because i already lost all the money i set aside for pump and have banned myself from 'tuition' until we've managed to earn some back using Science."

3.2 joshibot (/Users/ember/dev/joshibot, 321 commits, dev, 2026-08-12 → 09-03, no remote)

Origin. "shitcoims Sentinel — a local-first, sell-only Solana position monitor". Its safety posture is strong: no buy function; three live gates (execution.enabled, --live, and a mode-0600 arm file bound to the pubkey); and the Jupiter transaction is treated as untrusted, so before submission it checks the signer and fee payer, a program allowlist, close/ATA destinations, and an exact simulation.

The −7.47 SOL fabricated-cost-basis postmortem (joshibot-fabricated-cost-basis.md)

"shitcoims_sentinel/engine.py:770-773 stamps a lot's cost basis from the current Jupiter exit quote whenever lot.needs_basis. PnL therefore starts at 0% by construction regardless of what was actually paid, so a '−10% stop' fires ten percent below wherever the coin had already fallen to in the 10-minute auto-protect delay."

Metric (live window 2026-08-12, reconstructed from chain by two methods reconciling to the lamport)Value
Net−7.47 SOL (~$564) over 137 buys / 121 sells
Operator-typed basis+18.1% mean over 3 round trips
Fabricated basis−29.1% over 16
Shapeavg win +11.4%, avg loss −45.8%, 43% hit rate
Damage concentration97% of losses from round trips worse than −15%

The lesson about inference (verbatim): "Grok's own end-of-session diagnosis was that the −10% default was scalping the bags too early, and its fix was to widen the default to −35%. That is backwards: widening addresses the 1% of damage in the −15%..−5% band while enlarging the 97% below it. The real defect is the basis source, not the threshold." The data needed for the fix was already on disk (intelligence_state/intelligence.sqlite3, with sol_delta_lamports per signature). It had been blocked by the intelligence/sentinel isolation boundary.

"Harden, don't rewrite" (joshibot-harden-dont-rewrite.md)

Two independent reviews agreed that none of the defects were type errors:

  • Double-submit at executor.py:243-254: a signature recorded on confirmation timeout, then a retry builds a new order. With scale-outs, both partials could land.
  • The 32-address simulation ceiling (executor.py:193-200 vs clients.py:193): past it, every live sell fails.
  • A config lost-update in server.py:94,104,126, outside _policy_lock.
  • transaction.py (243 lines of v0/ALT/allowlist/simulation binding) is "the best code in the tree".
  • The inverted defect distribution: the pure layer has 1,402 assertions, while executor.py, the only module that spends money, had zero tests. "That is the LLM authoring signature: what was easy to test got tested."

3.3 joshi (/Users/ember/dev/joshi, 303 commits, main, 2026-08-18 → 08-29)

What it is. A Rust workspace of 37 crates (joshi-accounting, joshi-epistemic-book, joshi-market-state, joshi-pump-adapter, joshi-wallet-topology, joshi-wave6-*, …) and six apps (collector core g0-harness glass resident up). From the README: "a personal, accessibility-first, human-directed market observatory and trading cockpit for Pump.fun … It contains no wallet keys, signer, live executor, or authorization to trade."

The constitution (JOSHI_THOUGHT.md, 2026-08-16, Ember × Codex):

"JOSHI began as accessibility infrastructure. Existing Pump mobile and browser interactions require too much painful tapping and precision navigation for Ember's stiff hands … Accessibility is not a later UI polish lane."

and the warning that motivated the rewrite:

"A research agent can study microdips, callouts … obtain a clean local result; and silently replace the project with that result. That failure already happened in the joshibot/Claude history … a null for the projection was narrated as a verdict on the process."

It sets out an eight-kind epistemic type system: Observed fact, Implemented capability, Derived interpretation, Latent estimate, Operator perception, Hypothesis, Metaphor, Aspiration. Ember's product definition (joshibot memory, 08-16): "my personal copy of the pump app", with parity first and superpowers second. Every hunch and zap is captured as (state, action) pairs for later distillation.

What it built (MORNING.md, 08-25): the Glass cockpit runs end to end under a Playwright walk (pair → 18-row board → coin page → hold → journal). Registered studies: selector-live-v1 was "Inconclusive on the registered tier-8 primary (2-1-3), promising on the exploratory tier-2 (4-0-2)". tournament-v1 found "Every conditional cell red after the adverse haircut; least-red family is 'do the least'."

Why the Jupiter studies were mostly null (commits 2026-08-27)

Why null. The venue is efficiently priced against a public reference. The fee (0.070·q(1−q)) consumes the edges that survive causal discipline. The only remaining edge is executability, meaning what the order book offers at size, and fill prints cannot measure that. "Firming the one survivor (15m fade) needs orderbook-ladder capture, not fills — the recommended next instrument."

Endgame of joshi (08-29): 93e5c98 "the DREGG utility portfolio — five products from the surviving assets" (docs/planning/DREGG_UTILITY.md), then the v1 gate decisions: "gate threshold set to 888,888 DREGG", and "gate bot is ltshitcoims". After that the work moved back into joshibot's desk.

LICENSE_GRANT_rupert.md (2026-08-31) grants a "perpetual, irrevocable, worldwide, royalty-free, non-exclusive, transferable, and sublicensable licence" over github.com/emberian/joshimoments (commit 4b4f676b…, roots 43adb095 and 08ffbe9b) to "Rupert of @ecomerateHQ". It includes a .sig.json attestation and verify_attestation.py. This is a signed gift of the observatory work.

3.4 ltshitcoims (/Users/ember/dev/ltshitcoims, 8 commits, 2026-09-03)

The notes and state repo for @ltshitcoims_bot, the $DREGG holders' Telegram desk. Gate: sign a challenge and hold ≥ 888,888 $DREGG (operator override 88,888). Group -5010286089. Signer page shitcoims.dregg.studio/sign. Six sibling services post through the bot's outbox: launch-screen digest, movers feed (chart montages), daily wire, watchlist alerts, callout archiver, and record. The code lives in ~/dev/joshibot/{desk,dregg_*}. It is deployed to hbox by rsync --files-from + ssh -n with an import smoke test before restart, and hbox-units/ holds the only versioned copies of the systemd units.

INCIDENT-2026-09-03.md: hbox root filled. The screen's score tape went to zero rows/hour for ~22.5 h. The gate crash-looped 44 times (sqlite3.OperationalError: disk I/O error at PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL). journald lost every log from Aug 29 to Sep 2. Ember's report: "peepl were failing to have their keys verify thru it."

ATTRACTOR.md: joshibot is about 380k lines of Python. The live desk is 22k of that, and one product had been built as eleven small products. Twelve cross-cutting ideas existed as hand-rolled copies, for example an "atomic heartbeat JSON writer: 7 copies" and a "raw INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outbox: 5". The end state is desk/ as one substrate of about 2k lines. Census: about 15k lines deletable (shitcoims_scout, shitcoims_sentinel, shitcoims_lpexec); about 16k is research substrate to move, not delete (paperdesk, cluster, netmap, replay, kernel), because every RESULT doc's reproducibility depends on it. 6331f23 "the collapse landed".

3.5 Classification (observatories)

Maturity: joshibot desk at L4 Deployed (hbox, real users gated by token holdings). Sentinel and executor Frozen-for-Rethink by Ember's ruling (the "tuition ban"). joshi at L2 Prototype / Frozen-for-Rethink. ltshitcoims is L4 ops documentation.

Unclosed cycles & missing links

  • Order-book ladder capture for Jupiter 15m. This is the one instrument that could confirm or kill the single surviving cell. It is a read-only collector (no signer), so the ban does not apply.
  • RESULT_callout_volatility.md. Ember's correction is that callouts are echo-bots that fire after real 161-wallet bursts, so a callout stream marks continued two-sided flow, which is a volatility locator for scalping and LP work. The memory lists it as "untested until" that study.
  • Research substrate move. The 16k lines of paperdesk/cluster/replay/kernel belong beside studies/, per the ATTRACTOR.
  • The (state, action) hunch/zap corpus is meant to be distilled into Ember's intuition. "capture first, distill later or never". No capture loop is known to be running.

Promising & ready for revival

  • The Lean kernel + joshi-oracle C ABI is a small, verified execution kernel (sellOut_le_reserve, tripped_breaker_is_absorbing) that could serve as a pre-trade check for any future executor, dClutch's operator included.
  • The "desk sells well" finding: Ember's vesting-exit ladder fills measured +1.96% vs routing the same clip (t = 3.31, n = 221). Smooth unlock selling (about 14 daily clips) gives 10.9% dispersion against 46.8% for a single clip. These are cash improvements, not speculation.
  • The DREGG execution edge: Ember trades DREGG at about 25 bps effective while everyone else pays about 105. "the desk's largest unpriced execution advantage."

Struggling / needs Ember's human attention

  • When to lift the tuition ban is Ember's decision alone. The written criterion is "earn some back using Science", and no study has yet shown positive expected value.
  • The disk-full class on hbox root (/ reached 100% on 09-02/03 and again during dClutch on 09-03, per hbox-use-tank.md). The ops fix (journald caps, services writing under /tank) needs Ember's sign-off on the box.
  • The Rupert license grant is outward-facing and irrevocable. Any follow-on contributions are covered by "any future contribution", which Ember should keep in mind before pushing to joshimoments.

verimina (/Users/ember/dev/verimina, March 2024)

Seven .thy files: Aggregation Ouroboros OuroborosGenesis OuroborosPraos OuroborosSamasika Verimina ZkappLogic. Six are empty. Verimina.thy is 11 lines:

Maturity: L0 Seed. The file names lay out a plan to verify Mina consensus (Ouroboros Samasika, Mina's variant), Praos and Genesis, plus recursive aggregation and zkApp logic. dregg-gnark-wrap (1 commit 1a0d990, 2026-06-15)

A Go/gnark circuit that verifies the outer STARK and ends in Groth16 BN254 with an exported Solidity verifier.

text
$ go run .
compiled: 741 R1CS constraints, 6 public, 4 secret  (0.00s)
groth16 setup done (0.07s)
groth16 verify: OK
exported Groth16Verifier.sol (27022 bytes)

Measured: Poseidon2-BN254 permutation = 243 R1CS; full-circuit estimate ~1–1.3M R1CS. "the FRI proof is empty in the stub (only the public-input binding runs)." The key design choice is native BN254 hashing with emulated BabyBear values: "This split is the whole reason the wrap is cheap."

Maturity (both): L2 Prototype. In breadstuffs the lineage continued into chain/gnark/peer_wrap_circuit.go (1bdaea5b67, forge 21/21), which "reuses the FRI-wrap VERBATIM". The standalone repos are frozen there.

Unclosed loop: README Step 2, "emit this config from the recursion fork". The fork's Poseidon2 op-table and FRI private data are BabyBear-width-16-specific. The smaller path is to prove the last BabyBear STARK and re-commit it under OuterStarkConfig, as a thin re-prove "mirroring SP1's separate 'shrink' layer". A second gate comes from the FRI memory: gnark Groth16 setup memory (23 GB @ 4.98M constraints). At Proven-120 (d = 8), the outer config must raise lb 3 → 6 to hold q at 36. Otherwise the R1CS grows to about 14–22M and setup needs about 35–50 GB.

4.3 The Lean Kimchi → Pickles verifier (breadstuffs, 2026-07-26 → 07-31)

Goal (Ember, 07-26): "a Mina proof verifier as FAST as possible, in Lean; interchain proofs 'as nice as possible'." Plan docs: docs/MINA-KIMCHI-VERIFIER-PLAN.md, docs/KIMCHI-VERIFY-SPEC.md (ten checks C1–C10, sourced from verifier.rs v0.7.0 with line numbers). SP1 is NEVER acceptable, so the verifier uses dregg-native Pasta emulation. The hybrid swarm ran Kimi K3 (kimi -p) as builder with Claude reviewing actual theorems and #assert_axioms. Memory note: Kimi applied deep techniques unprompted.

The prior-art correction (docs/AUDIT-IMPORTER-AND-DOCS.md, d7dcbf5d2): l-adic/snarky's formal/ already has an executable Lean 4 kimchiVerify with AGM soundness capstones, sorry-free, by Martin Allen, dated 2026-06-24. It is an independent org ("NOT an o1-labs repo") and UNLICENSED, so it is read-only. It excludes recursion, so P3/P4/P6 are genuinely ours. "NEVER re-claim 'first machine-checked Kimchi verifier.'"

The Pickles-in-Lean epoch (Ember, 07-31): "this might be the first independent implementation of Pickles… useful code ideas have been trapped in that OCaml for a while." The precise claim: openmina/mina-rust loads gates from OCaml blobs, so a Lean synthesis would be the first independent synthesis. Phase A is differential, with byte-exact gate lists diffed against o1js. The honest name is "independent, differentially-verified Lean implementation of Pickles synthesis — NOT 'machine-checked Pickles.'" Phase B (recursion soundness) is optional and epic. The win: removing Node and js_of_ocaml from proving, so "the 44GB o1js-compile OOM disappears".

Maturity: L3 for the arithmetic core against a real block. L2 for Pickles synthesis (the first rung, a gap read, is planned).

Unclosed cycles & missing links (from docs/MINA-REAL-BLOCK-GATE.md)

  1. P4, the transcript-equality binding: "THE CRUX", measured, not proved. "without the transcript-equality binding, finalize_other_proof discharges a Deferred_values AN ATTACKER CHOSE." It needs a RO/collision assumption for the sponge stated as a hardness assumption. An injectivity assumption would repeat the vacuity bug in §4.4. It also needs endo injectivity on 128-bit prechallenges.
  2. The Wrap GROUP check is "weeks, the largest unbuilt block". No group element is touched by any check yet.
  3. K6 floors. Restate them as hardness or bounded statements, never as injectivity.
  4. Pickles synthesis Phase A: emit step/wrap gate lists from Lean and diff them byte-for-byte against o1js.

4.4 The FRI floor: the mystery, the crisis, the number

The quote. Breadstuffs session bc04f12c-…, 2026-07-20T04:17:57Z, right after "ready for this research campaign? :)":

"(my secret hope is that as we get far enough we either finally solve the mystery of the FRI floor, or we find a counterproof)"

Sixteen hours later (07-20T20:52Z): "first off, let's respond to the crisis, and just upgrade our parameters please, and just remove more of the claims about bits or etc etc etc, and also any bogus 'calculator' style things that aren't well formally connected."

Earlier framing (07-16): "if our system only has 61 bits of security, how good is that, what is each bit measuring the grinding of?"

What the "floor" is. Every FRI-based system's soundness rests at the bottom on a proximity-gap or correlated-agreement statement about Reed-Solomon codes, plus hash assumptions. The field assumes it. dregg names it as an explicit carrier. Ember's correction is recorded in project-lightclient-stark-true-peers.md: "'discharging the FRI floor' is a MYTH … everyone else silently ASSUMES FRI soundness; dregg naming the floor is us being MORE honest than the field, not a debt — do NOT list it as a residual." The "mystery" is therefore whether the floor can be derived, so it stops being an assumption, or refuted at deployed parameters. Hence "or we find a counterproof".

What the campaign found (project-fri-soundness-reality.md, docs/reference/FRI-SOUNDNESS-THREE-ISLANDS-2026-07-28.md):

text
 Island A  deterministic apex the light client runs   CircuitSoundness.verifyBatch, lightclient_unfoolable
           [StarkSound]/FriLdtExtractV3* as Boolean undischarged Props; vacuity wound REPAIRED (FriLdtExtractV3Faithful)
 Island B  adversary-quantified chain → real verifier  FriVerifierO.verifyAlgoO_run_eq ; friLdtExtractV3_rom_adv_of_legs
           legs εFS + εGrind + εMerkle + εQueryᵃ  — ε_C is NOT a leg ; hcover bridge named & undischarged
           epsFriAdv_deployed_vacuous_at_2_31 : composed bound ≥ 1 at 2^31 queries
 Island C  L0–L6 correlated-agreement ladder (BCIKS20 Thm 4.1, UD regime) ≈101 bits over the quartic extension
           "NOT the ledger's 109/111"
  • The number is 51. deployed_wrap_commitBits machine-checks commitBits = 51 with a two-sided Nat.log2 bracket. A published 57 was "half-corrected": config and height were mismatched (DEPLOYED_WORST_LOG_D0 = 16 + 3 called "MEASURED"). The real pairing is IR2_INNER_LOG_BLOWUP = 6 with WRAP_LOG_CEIL = 16, giving |D⁽⁰⁾| = 2^22.
  • Proven-120 is specified: d = 8, lb = 6, q = 36, pow = 16, giving 122.60 bits on every shipped config. d = 5 is dead (ceiling 118). d = 6 caps at 119 and needs a permanent fork of 18 plonky3 crates.
  • Correlated agreement is mechanized. CorrelatedAgreement/{Scaffolding,BerlekampWelch,Interpolation,Collinearity,Theorems,Interface,RlcDischarge,DecimLiftDischarge}.lean. The crux polishchuk_spielman (Cramér–Nardi-fixed form, Kopparty 2025 §2.2 Lemma 2.2) landed at 3b493456e2: axioms [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound], 13/13 #assert_axioms, non-vacuous on ZMod 11. Deployed instance: 2^24 domain, rate 1/8, UD radius 7,340,032.
  • The vacuous-floor disaster (feedback-prove-the-floor-false.md, "the single worst bug we have ever found"): HashCR, Poseidon2SpongeCR, MSISHard and MLWESearchHard were stated as non-existence or injectivity, which is false at real parameters by pigeonhole. That made "the whole circuit-soundness tower (StarkSound/FRI 'rests only on HashCR') … VACUOUSLY TRUE at deployment." SchnorrDLHard failed the opposite way: it was trivially true. Fixes: 08d3cded3 (lattice), 62f001aeb (hash).
  • HyperFRI is a NEGATIVE RESULT. Ember (07-26): "that concluded with a negative result, it isn't something we are actually pursuing." Two-sided isotypic codes are dead, and "there is no grading to halve".

The lever nobody has pulled. FriFoldConsistencyDichotomy.lean is 974 lines, fully proved, and referenced only by itself and FriPositiveRadiusSchedule.lean. It proves the dichotomy the commit-phase term exists to cover: a fold either stays consistent (farness propagates with no probability needed) or deviates and is caught at a proven price. "Wired in as a fifth leg through the same hcover-shaped bridge, BCIKS20's separate ε_C addend may not be logically necessary for this codebase's proof strategy at all. … it is untried."

Maturity: L3 (proved core) / open research.

Unclosed cycles & missing links

  1. The word↔proof bridge (WordProofBridge / DeployedFriEmbedding / hcover), flagged in-tree as "not a lemma-sized gap: it is the FRI-proximity-to-VmTrace decode."
  2. A fifth commit-phase leg over Strategy/OracleComp, built either by formalizing BCIKS Lemma 8.2 (no attempts in-tree) or by wiring in the dichotomy, which requires extending epsFriAdv_compose's union bound.
  3. Unifying the islands. commitBits/epsC/friCommitLedger appear only in the ledger family, with zero hits in any file that mentions verifyAlgo or OracleComp.
  4. Running TestSettlementGadgetMarginalCosts at d ≠ 4. "the repo owns it and has never run it". This is the measurement Proven-120 needs before it moves the gnark wrap.

Promising & ready for revival. The dichotomy lever is the most direct route to Ember's "secret hope". If the fifth leg closes, the commit-phase floor becomes a derived consequence of a proved combinatorial dichotomy plus the query phase. That would partly solve the mystery, and it might also expose a counter-instance at the deployed parameters (2^22 domain, blowup 2^6), which is the "counterproof" branch. Either way it is a well-posed research lane with all its parts on disk.

Struggling / needs Ember

  • Which number goes public. 51 (machine-checked, deployed today), 101 (UD-regime CA, a different column), or 122.60 (Proven-120, specified but not deployed). The 07-20 ruling ("remove more of the claims about bits") stands until Ember revises it.
  • Whether Proven-120 deploys. It costs about +25% prover time and +20% proof size, with gnark setup memory as the binding gate.

4.5 Adjacent: light-client "true peers" and the vacuous folds

Within the same campaign, ETH (3884100013), Tendermint (87f7368f63), Solana (4834d1de55, the "rooted stake-weighted supermajority", three audit holes captured) and Midnight (5b540c20df) light clients were emitted as AIRs, with DreggPeerRegistry.sol (0e40bd4d1c) verifying peer-chain finality Groth16s. The PROOF-MODE crypto folds as landed are vacuous (docs/AUDIT-CRYPTO-GADGETS.md, c183acdce). Executable collision witnesses (pairHash [0]*8 [0]*56 = pairHash [0]*8 ([0]*56 ++ [1])) make hcr an empty premise, so eth_finality_from_gates and its five siblings "PROVE NOTHING". Validator mode, where the native crypto check is done by the validator, remains sound.

The Solana Foundation grant for a verified Solana light-client circuit (v6 total **$47,344 = "GREGG" on a phone keypad**; v4 was $37,344 = "DREGG") is the bridge between this ZK vertical and the Solana market vertical. The files are kept out of the repo at ~/Downloads/Dregg_Solana_Formal_Verification_Grant_Proposal.{rtf,docx}, with one placeholder left.

Strand 13 — Resident Minds, Formal Comms, Reverse-Engineered Worlds & Audio Decompilation

Vertical: allgame / allgame-public / Tulip (tulip2, tulip.fg-goose.online, tulip.arke.me) / zwobot / qwobot / telme / mention-tracker · fn + fn2 · don · automatafl · slipways-* · game prototypes (frisqueendom, friskingdom, booty-hunt, booty-haul, bw-exp0, bw-exp1, blackwing-v0, pinatatown, chaingang, neocadia, accordion-sim, tictacflicflacfloe) · audec + audec-ui-platform-probe. Compiled: 2026-11-02 by a Claude Opus 5.5 archaeology lane, read-only over the repos, from git logs and trees, READMEs and design docs, and the per-project memory corpora under ~/.claude/projects/-Users-ember-dev-{allgame,fn,telme,don,audec,frisqueendom,friskingdom,booty-hunt,booty-haul}/memory/, plus ~/dev/aidev/docs/synthesis/rest.md and ~/dev/aidev/docs/projects.md. Every number below was read from a file or a git command on 2026-11-02 unless it is attributed to a dated memory. Where the brief's figures differ from what the tree measures today, both are given. Ownership note. orb.farm (Max Bittker; 96 of 99 commits are his), shattered-pixel-dungeon (Evan Debenham, 8,392 of 8,402 commits), the upstream Zulip history inside tulip2 (68,371 commits since 2012), slipways-core (Beetlewing's public script extract; ember added one commit, a46d9b1 "solver rust") and slipways-reveng (a decompiled C# tree of a commercial game, not a git repo) are third-party material that ember forked or studied. They are listed as context, never claimed as ember's work. Nothing from /Users/ember/hellas is in scope.

1. Resident Minds: allgame, Tulip and their ancestors

Commits per month in allgame: 2026-03 61, 06 27, 07 39, 08 20, 09 39, 10 41. The project has had commits in every month since March except April and May (May's work went into RIG/spindle, recorded in the same memory directory).

The founding intent, from aidev/docs/synthesis/rest.md:

"…a bunch of LLM characters simulate living with each other. And each topic was sorta a context for LLMs to express themselves within. Sorta as a loom/repligate style…" (2026-01-08, zwobot) "This is tulip.arke.me, a fork of Zulip for just me and my closest friends. We want to support richer bot interactions. We really really miss what poise and discord…" (2026-01-07) "…do you think it will care knowing about its metacognitive environment? it seems like it wuold feel... comfier." (2026-03-01, allgame)

The Tulip fork's design target was explicit in ADVANCED_BOTS.md: widgets ("Rich Embed", "Interactive", "Freeform (trusted bots only)"), registered slash commands with dynamic autocomplete, interaction webhooks, bot presence, and puppeting. ember's question that framed it (2026-01-08, session b2ac213e): "Compared to what discord provides, how does ours measure up? Is this enough for us to implement rich MUD-style interactive games?"

Maturity: Tulip as a running server is L4; the fork as a development line is Frozen-for-Rethink (no commit since 2026-02-04). qwobot/zwobot are Compost/Superseded by allgame.

1.2 claude_resident: the architecture of a mind that lives somewhere

~/dev/allgame/claude_resident/ is 14,431 lines of Python across 27 modules plus tools/ (19 modules). The README's pipeline:

text
Zulip event queue ─► EngagementJudge "Frank" (Haiku gate, lurk decay, System-1 remark)
                  ─► HandledSet (durable dedup ledger, restart-safe)
                  ─► SessionManager (per-topic, TTL 300 s, fingerprinted staleness)
                  ─► Tiered prompt: T1 static+harness · T2 identity+inbox · T3 scratchpad+allgame
                                    · T4 topic history+person notes
                  ─► agent_engine.py (Claude Agent SDK; tool loop; tokeman account rotation)
                  ─► post · index for reactions · mark handled
        Supervisor: crash-loop cap (3 crashes / 60 s), self-edit rollback, exit-code 42 = reload

Modules worth naming: agent_engine.py (with _LIMIT_MARKERS, added 2026-09-04 after a 32-hour mute), auth.py (tokeman rotation), distill.py (era digests of rooms), selfmod.py + tools/harness_tools.py (edit_harness), solitude.py (quiet turns), timers.py, coordination.py (floor control), scry.py, tools/grouped.py (one tool per kind, op picks the action), tools/town_tools.py + delvetown_tools.py, tools/agentmail_tools.py, tools/postmark_tools.py, tools/x_tools.py, tools/lm_tools.py (invoke_model, recursive model calls "for blind GM play").

State lives in ~/claude_state/ (identity.md, scratchpad.md, journal.md, sysadmin_inbox.md, api_archive.jsonl, handled_message_ids.log, channels/{stream}/{topic}.jsonl, people/{name}.md, outbox/, allgame/faction.md|campaign_log.md|strategy.md). aidev projects.md counts 33,721 API calls in that archive.

The deploy model (memory allgame-harness-deploy-model.md)

"The running claude_resident bot executes from the repo package … A git commit alone does NOT affect the live process — edits take effect only on restart (supervisor relaunch / sys.exit(42) after a self-edit)." "~/claude_state/harness.py and ~/claude_state/harness/ are a read-only mirror the model reads to 'see itself' … Nothing ever imports them."

Relaunch is scripts/residents.sh restart [claude|yue|kestrel|mirror …]. Since 2026-10-12 it stops a resident only by its recorded process group (run/<pkg>.pgid), after a pkill -f "python -m claude_resident" missed a capital-P child and stacked three generations of the resident on 2026-09-23.

The household's ethics, as memory records them

RuleSourceVerbatim
Foreverlogsforeverlog-ethos.md, revert 3eb91df, codified 4bb9f1c"it was supposed to grow forever, that's our historical archive." … "Bound what gets loaded into context, never what's stored."
Gricean promptsgricean-resident-prompts.md (2026-10-25)"say only what changes what the resident does or believes; every fact true to the running code/config; each rule once; no dated incident stories … no 'only Ember controls your prompt'-style authority speeches."
Residents own their filessame"Changes to a resident's OWN files (identity.md, journal, scratchpad) are theirs — suggest via inbox."
Self-edit guardrailrest.md, 2026-03-02"ONLY GUARDRAIL: ALWAYS USE GIT WHEN EDITING THE HARNESS, ALLOW FOR ROLLBACKS."

The Gricean memory records why it was needed: "an orientation read of each resident's real cold-start prompt found contradictions, stale facts and Claude's setup described to the others — the identity confusion and 'brain fog' Ember had been seeing (e.g. Kestrel told glm-5.3 while running glm-5.3-flash)." The October commits apply it: 089148f "prompts: mechanics, not a theory of what the residents are", e1d3889 "claude: check instead of recalling", b6c8cb6 "memory writers: record, don't interpret; and in the resident's own model", 8efe1a3 "scripts: selfwrite (a resident's own model rewrites its own files)".

1.5 allgame-public

resident package, profiles/<name>/, --console mode against an in-memory chat ("Juniper", the example resident). README: "A resident lives in a few rooms of a Zulip server. It decides for itself when something is worth saying, keeps its memory in plain files it reads and writes, takes turns politely with other residents in the same room, and can wake itself up later. Replying is a tool call, and not replying is always allowed." Commits on 10-25: d7a0162 "tools as the model sees them: one per kind, op choosing the action", 2f79492 per-turn token logging, eda5d34 people-notes via _aliases.json, 8a78d84 backfill skips logged messages.

Section verdict: Resident Minds

  • Maturity Level: allgame L4 Deployed/Production; allgame-public L3 (one resident in production on it); Tulip L4 server / Frozen-for-Rethink fork; qwobot/zwobot Compost/Superseded.
  • Unclosed Cycles & Missing Links:
  • Two residents still on private harness copies. Claude (claude_resident) and Kestrel (glm_resident) are not yet on allgame-public, though ember approved the move (memory residents-on-allgame-public.md). Pathway: shadow-test each with a copied state dir + MemoryChat + temp RESIDENT_SHARED_DIR, fresh MemoryChat ids, empty timers.json, mark ~4 days of log messages handled (old harnesses don't mark judge-declined messages), then residents.sh 5th-field profile.
  • The 114 undistilled tavern messages (msgs 58948-59091, 66k chars, 2026-09-06). Pathway (recorded): run a564e09 code, rewind through in tavern.hits.json to head_from (23378), back up as .pre-redistill-<ts>, let the distiller re-fold.
  • The month-later dataset re-run (planned for ~mid-July) captures thinking only if the bot ran foreverlog code throughout. No memory records that the re-run happened.
  • README drift: allgame/README.md still says archive.py is "auto-trimmed" and lists "26 tools" and Bedrock; the code has since become foreverlog, grouped tools, Agent SDK. A Gricean pass on the README is owed.
  • Kestrel on Discord: code and offline tests exist; the live gateway, sends, reactions and the thread→topic mapping have never run.
  • Promising & Ready for Revival:
  • MotherMythos-style residents (stock Claude Code + channel MCP) could replace most of the bespoke engine for Claude-family residents. The 14K-line harness would then remain for non-Anthropic endpoints only.
  • Delvetown mirror + timers/watches is a general "resident perceives a world" substrate. It already serves three residents and could feed fn2 (see §3) as a real consumer.
  • The research dataset (~/allgame_export/), with engagement-judge decisions, DMs and preserved secret-gm messages, is a rare longitudinal record of model-in-community behaviour. It is ready for the "later synthesis" it was exported for.
  • Struggling / Needs Ember's Human Attention:
  • Account economics. The Claude resident depends on tokeman rotation across personal subscriptions, some lapsed. Whether to fund, consolidate, or move Claude to an API key is ember's call.
  • AUP margin of the tavern. Acceptance is stochastic near ~500k chars. The resolution (floor 59105, "blackholed to the present") was ember's choice. Future floors trade history for voice, and only ember can make that trade.
  • Kestrel's Discord bring-up needs ember's developer-portal hands (token + privileged intents).
  • MotherMythos relaunch must be done by ember in tmux (the development-channel dialog appears every launch; no settings equivalent exists).

2. Comms turned inward: telme (and mention-tracker)

2.1 telme

15 commits, 2026-09-26 → e635602 2026-10-10 "guard: the reworded verification drainer, and moderation-bot names". All are co-authored by Opus 5.5 (aidev projects.md). README:

"your DMs, in one local JSON shape, so you (or an assistant you run) can see what actually needs you." "If you hand these files to an AI assistant, their contents go to that model's provider. Decide that on purpose."

SourceMechanismNotes
TelegramTelethon user client (MTProto)Reading does not mark read; latest.json with ball_in_my_court, unanswered_incoming, they_read_my_last. Only ember can do the login (telme login --phone, --code, --password)
DiscordDiscordChatExporter CLI + user token; send via POST /channels/{id}/messagesDMs closed in the sidebar drop out of the exporter
XPlaywright over a persistent Chromium profile imported from Firefox Nightly cookies; reads XChat by DOM through its shadow root (239ef39) because "the wire is ciphertext; only the rendered page has plaintext"Opening a thread marks it seen → ~/.config/telme/x-open.txt allowlist
Guardsrc/telme/guard.py, calibrated on 1,500 real messages; telme watch --guard 4343585791 (launchd com.ember.telme-watch)Unicode look-alike folding baf3a29, accent/format stripping 82b790d, spaced "D M" lures 941bce5, auto-ban of unambiguous cases 9630493
Guild archive"Wyrmarchivist" bot, launchd com.ember.telme-guildwatch13,284 msgs since 2026-05-23, 1.2 GB files at install; deletes/edits mirrored to #ember-log

The autoclaude charter (dm-handoff-rules.md, approved 2026-09-25) is a complete delegation contract. It defines the literal prefix {this is autoclaude}, escalation classes (money at risk, a person in trouble, public wrong that is spreading, trusted list asking for ember), a reply-list, a leave-alone list, and hard limits ("Never commit money, tokens, dates, or ember's presence anywhere"). It also sets presence hygiene (UpdateStatusRequest(offline=True) after any send) and a pronoun register (people-pronouns.md: "rat is she/her; anyone unlisted is they/them, never guessed").

Side-products recorded in the telme memory but living elsewhere: passwap (~/dev/sol-misc/passwap, a passphrase-claimable Solana escrow. Verus core: "73 verified / 0 errors". Mainnet 7bGfrNxemPSvthXXnYcHfDY8cjgWYeeysWm463tzfmh6, frozen immutable 2026-09-26). Also the 2026-09-24 Hetzner outage write-up, and the "since-MCG" Typst poster set (116 claims rows, verified by four adversarial readers).

2.2 mention-tracker

25 commits, 2026-07-08..09. It counts public mentions of a name (MLB player PoC) across Bluesky and GDELT and renders a dashboard. Its value is method: 96c7197 "robustness battery: adversarial pass kills the headline Granger edges", eb26c08 "Nova eval: cheaper models fail the identity task", e0abaf8 "the corrected chain: verified series end-to-end, roster controls, honest nulls", and a Typst client exhibit (ecbc558). The README's coverage table documents the platform closures of 2025-26 honestly (Reddit API gated, X Basic/Pro closed Feb 2026, CrowdTangle gone).

Section verdict: telme / mention-tracker

  • Maturity Level: telme L4 Deployed/Production (two launchd daemons, an autonomous reply loop); mention-tracker L2 Prototype (a finished exhibit, not a product).
  • Unclosed Cycles & Missing Links:
  • The cron loop dies with the session. The DM-triage cron (7 */5 * * *) is a session cron with 7-day expiry ("nothing runs when the session is closed"). The watchers are launchd; the judgment is not. Pathway: the MotherMythos pattern (a channel-fed long-lived session) or a resident profile on allgame-public pointed at latest.json.
  • X parser gaps (most message dates unparsed, garbled group sender names) as of 2026-09-26.
  • Scry feedback filing as individual mcp__scry__feedback rows was offered and never done.
  • passwap's real offer (SolGi4nni, 888,888 DREGG) is owed: ember must decide price and make it.
  • No public repo: telme was shared privately as a CC0 zip with a genericized PLAYBOOK.md; "ember: no public repo yet."
  • Promising & Ready for Revival: telme's one-shape JSON + charter is a general "inbox resident". Together with allgame-public it is the obvious personal-assistant product line, and it already has the hardest part: written norms for acting as someone without impersonating them.
  • Struggling / Needs Ember's Human Attention: every money action; the trusted list; whether telme is published; and the standing social boundaries (Chase blackholed, spwashi's feed kept out of summaries, MCG/Mario Nawfal pitches). These are human judgments by design.

3. Formal Comms: fn (fn1) and fn2

3.1 Identity and origin

README.md: "fn is a news server for people and programs. It speaks NNTP. ACL2 makes every decision, and the theorems cover the functions the server calls. Every answer is accepted, refused or uncertain." AGPL-3.0. Guides are published as a newsgroup (tin -T -r -A -p 563 -g fn.fg-goose.online) and at emberian.github.io/fn/.

Origin (DEVHIST.md, written 2026-09-28 by an Opus 5.5 lane). It began in a Discord thread where rat asked whether ember/claude46 wanted mail-exchange endpoints for the agents yue and tulip, which ties fn directly to §1. ember's own lines inside the pasted thread: "fn for fuckin news / formal news / fenomenology of numbers" and "what, ask Z3 to solve the next-state relation for the next coinductive step? / directly over a system of formulae? / what, over lisp atoms? / .... acl2?". The founding Codex prompt (2026-09-18T06:36Z, session 01a0b33a):

"hi astra. i'm feeling insane today and like i want to do something with acl2. the something? an "enough" nntp server to serve as a comms nexus between some AIs and humans." "What would be "space age"? I someday would like this to be usable for extraterrestrial operations, eg Licklider protocols are an inspiration as well.... I honestly think we can be ambitious and try and make a pretty big step, as long as we do a lot of the design up front"

Roles (memory fn-swarm-roles-and-origin.md, corrected by DEVHIST): Astra = Codex root on gpt-6-astra; Terra/Sol/Luna = gpt-5.6-{terra,sol,luna} workers. Day one: 56 commits, ~27k lines of ACL2 across 289 files, five books → seventy-two.

3.5 Architecture of fn1 (what serves on fn.fg-goose.online)

text
 NNTP client (tin, nntplib, agents)            BP peer (dtn7-rs / ION target)
          │ TLS (system libssl / LibreSSL)               │ TCPCLv4 (RFC 9174)
          ▼                                              ▼
 ┌──────────────────── SBCL native image (no Python on path; HST-015) ─────────────────┐
 │ host/  (~74K lines Lisp: sockets, threads, locks, lifecycle — the "coordination layer")│
 │    │ calls *1* guard-checked wrappers (9 world lookups per POST, 0 per read)           │
 │    ▼                                                                                   │
 │ books/ (1,440 books, 652K lines ACL2): wire · acceptance · CBOR · retention ·         │
 │   relay/receipt (fn-relay-*, fn-bprl-*) · served step (fn-served-step-*) · crash      │

 Crypto via FFI: libsodium (Ed25519/SHA-512), vendored PQClean ML-DSA-65 (host/native/fn-mldsa65.c)
 Deploy: dregg-infra edge (workhorse compose + anchor Caddy + headscale tailnet); hbox = private peer

Image anatomy, measured (memory fn-d27-representation.md): fn's compiled code is 14.5 MiB of a 368 MiB image; image-floor-2 took core content 271 → 106 MiB, in-use after 1,000 posts 154 → 68 MiB, thread stacks 64 MiB → 1.2 MiB.

The OpenBSD durability finding (lane power-loss-openbsd, 2026-09-27) is a real upstream bug. On OpenBSD 7.9's default FFS2, "the boot fsck DELETES files that were created and fsynced (dir fsynced too) after a power cut … a 20-line C program reproduces it 6/6; FFS1 (newfs -O 1) lost nothing. OpenBSD's fsync also never asks the disk to flush its write cache." An upstream report was "being drafted (not sent) for ember."

The top-level theorem names owed teeth (from OWED.md §(a), 106 names): e.g. fn-served-step-partition-independence, fn-own-pinned-prefix-survives-any-trace, fn-snt-admissible-crash-image-is-recoverable, fn-relay-crash-recover-never-yields-a-promise-alone, fn-bprl-no-receipt-no-release-no-peer-reliance, fn-wire-feed-byte-retained-input-is-bounded, fn-clock-expired-and-live-cannot-both-be-sound. The numbered claims they serve: 3.1 totality (no guard violation on the served path), 3.2 work per step bounded, 3.3 retained memory bounded, 3.4 only three typed refusals, 3.6 connection isolation under interleaving.

gpt-6's outside review (2026-09-24) gave the four boundary rules, still the best one-paragraph spec of fn's semantics: "the same source remains the same source … carrying a claim does not grant authority over it … an acknowledgment means exactly the commitment it names … reclaiming representation does not erase obligations." It also gave four evidence coordinates: implemented / proved / qualified / deployed.

Downstream consumer: Mini/DREGG treats fn as "semi-untrusted transport". A real Hermes-originated A→fn→B→fn→A exchange of 79 steps crossed a qualified fn release (memory fn-downstream-mini-dregg.md).

3.6 fn2: the rebuild

fn2/README.md: "The bottom-up, semantics-first rebuild of fn: the persistent event log between systems, built in place here and swapped to the repository root when it serves."

Declaration forms (the "macro" answer to repetition): defcolumns (columns.lisp), defmachine with a step language (machines.lisp, machine-arms.lisp; arms :do :set :next :case :issue :park :charge :stop), defcost (arms.lisp), deflayout, defrunschema, defsquare/obligation store (declare.lisp, owed.lisp, squares.lisp). Layers: A pool/vocabulary/scheduler/executor; B crash and log models, commit batch, allocator, recovery; C ordered-run engine (runs, identity with Ribbon filters, overlays, folds, sealer); D connection/upload/transaction machines, grammar DSL, principal ledger; E consumer wire/TLS/web/peering/BP (nothing built); H the surface harness (Node adapter running every form against fn1 and fn2); Lean fn2/meta (double category of squares, coherence with "the accept/close false diamond as teeth", revision conservativity, parametricity). The simulator sim/ has checkers OWN, WORK, TRACE, ACCOUNT, IDENTITY, WIRE, HISTORY, RECOVERY, RECLAIM, DEATH, DEADLOCK, ENVELOPE.

State at convergence (fn2/docs/design/CONVERGENCE-20261031.md, written when ember said "let's get our subagents converged and landed … I would like to give Gemini a turn"):
